<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Usman bin Abul Aas (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain ki jab mujhe Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Taif ka aamil muqarrar kiya, toh mujhe namaz mein kuch idhar udhar ka khayal aane laga yahan tak ki mujhe yaad nahi rehta ki main kya padhta hoon. Jab maine ye halat dekhi toh main safar karke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki khidmat mein pahuncha, toh aapne farmaya: Kya ibn-e-Abul Aas ho? Maine kaha: Ji haan, Allah ke Rasool! Aapne sawal kiya: Tum yahan kyun aaye ho? Maine arz kiya: Allah ke Rasool! Mujhe namaz mein tarah tarah ke khayalat aate hain yahan tak ki mujhe ye bhi khabar nahi rehti ki main kya padh raha hoon. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ye shaitan hai, tum mere qareeb aao. Main aapke qareeb hua, aur apne paon ki ungliyon par do-zanu baith gaya. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne apne mubarak hath se mera seena thapathapaya aur apne munh ka lu&#x27;ab mere munh mein dala, aur farmaya: &#x27;Ukhruj aduwwallah&#x27; (Allah ke dushman! Nikal ja). Ye amal aapne teen baar kiya, iske baad mujhse farmaya: Apne kaam par jao. Usman (Radi Allahu Anhu) bayan karte hain: Qasam se! Mujhe nahi maloom ki phir kabhi shaitan mere qareeb bhatka ho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3548]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/3548" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Ibn Majah   3548 ]</p>

## Narration 1148

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-ibnmajah%3A3550)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 32</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، قَالَتْ صَلَّى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ فِي خَمِيصَةٍ لَهَا أَعْلاَمٌ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ شَغَلَنِي أَعْلاَمُ هَذِهِ اذْهَبُوا بِهَا إِلَى أَبِي جَهْمٍ وَائْتُونِي بِأَنْبِجَانِيَّتِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3550]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/3550" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Ibn Majah · 3550</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Ibn Majah   3550 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that ‘Aishah said:“The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) prayed in a Khamisah* that had markings on it. Then he said: ‘These markings distracted me. Take it to Abu Jahm and bring me an Anbijaniyyah.’”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3550]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/3550" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Ibn Majah · 3550</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Ibn Majah   3550 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्मुल मोमिनीन आयशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) कहती हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने एक चादर में नमाज़ पढ़ी जिसमें नक़्श-ओ-निगार थे, तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: मुझे इन बैल-बूटों ने ग़ाफ़िल कर दिया, इसे अबुल जहम के पास लेकर जाओ और मुझे उनकी अम्बिजानी चादर ला कर दे दो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3550]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/3550" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام المؤمنین عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا کہتی ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ایک چادر میں نماز پڑھی جس میں نقش و نگار تھے، تو آپ صلی اللہعلیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: مجھے ان بیل بوٹوں نے غافل کر دیا، اسے ابوجہم کو کے پاس لے کر جاؤ اور مجھے ان کی انبجانی چادر لا کر دے دو ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3550]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/3550" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

## Narration 1149

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-ibnmajah%3A3552)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 32</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ الْجَحْدَرِيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ الأَحْوَصِ بْنِ حَكِيمٍ، عَنْ خَالِدِ بْنِ مَعْدَانَ، عَنْ عُبَادَةَ بْنِ الصَّامِتِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ صَلَّى فِي شَمْلَةٍ قَدْ عَقَدَ عَلَيْهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3552]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/3552" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Ibn Majah · 3552</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from ‘Ubadah bin Samit that the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) prayed in a Shamlah tied with a knot</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3552]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/3552" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उबड़ा बिन समित (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने एक ऐसी चादर में नमाज़ पढ़ी जिस में आप ने गिरह लगा रखी थी.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3552]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/3552" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3552]</p>

## Narration 1150

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-ibnmajah%3A3553)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 32</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يُونُسُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الأَعْلَى، حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ وَهْبٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ إِسْحَاقَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي طَلْحَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، قَالَ كُنْتُ مَعَ النَّبِيِّ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ وَعَلَيْهِ رِدَاءٌ نَجْرَانِيٌّ غَلِيظُ الْحَاشِيَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3553]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/3553" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Anas bin Malik said:“I was with the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) and over him was a Najrani upper wrap with a thick border.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3553]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/3553" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अनस बिन मालिक (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं के मैं नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ था पर मोटे किनारे वाली नजरनि चादर थी.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3553]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">انس بن مالک رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ میں نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ تھا، آپ کے جسم پر موٹے کنارے والی نجرانی چادر تھی۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 3553]</p>
